# Now let's identify the chambers and verify the line graph structure
# We need to classify each state by its chamber (permutation of digits)

def classify_chamber(g):
    """Classify the chamber by computing the digit ordering"""
    g1, g2, g3 = g
    # Use canonical d4=0 to get the digits
    d4 = 0
    d3 = g3
    d2 = g3 + g2
    d1 = g3 + g2 + g1
    
    # Compute Kaprekar step to get the result digits
    n = int(f"{d1}{d2}{d3}{d4}")
    k = kaprekar(n)
    if k == 0:
        return None
    
    d = sorted(digits(k), reverse=True)
    # The unsorted digits (before sorting) are the F_i values
    # But we need to know which F_i corresponds to which position
    # Let's compute the raw subtraction result
    
    # Actually, let's compute the four digit forms directly
    # D = d1 d2 d3 d4, A = d4 d3 d2 d1
    # We need to do the subtraction with borrows
    
    # Let's do it explicitly
    D = [d1, d2, d3, d4]
    A = [d4, d3, d2, d1]
    
    # Subtraction with borrows
    result = []
    borrow = 0
    for i in range(3, -1, -1):  # right to left
        diff = D[i] - A[i] - borrow
        if diff < 0:
            diff += 10
            borrow = 1
        else:
            borrow = 0
        result.insert(0, diff)
    
    # result is [k1, k2, k3, k4] (thousands to units)
    # Now sort to get the permutation
    sorted_result = sorted(result, reverse=True)
    
    # Find permutation: which original index maps to which sorted position
    # indices: 0=thousands, 1=hundreds, 2=tens, 3=units
    perm = []
    used = [False] * 4
    for val in sorted_result:
        for i in range(4):
            if not used[i] and result[i] == val:
                perm.append(i)
                used[i] = True
                break
    
    return tuple(perm), tuple(result)

# Classify all states
chamber_groups = defaultdict(list)
for g in all_gaps:
    chamber_info = classify_chamber(g)
    if chamber_info:
        perm, raw_digits = chamber_info
        chamber_groups[perm].append((g, raw_digits))

print(f"Number of chambers (permutations): {len(chamber_groups)}")
for perm, states in sorted(chamber_groups.items(), key=lambda x: len(x[1]), reverse=True):
    print(f"  Permutation {perm}: {len(states)} states")
    # Show first state and its image
    g, raw = states[0]
    img = transitions[g]
    print(f"    Example: {g} -> {img}, raw digits={raw}")Number of chambers (permutations): 10

# Let's verify the line graph structure and slope restriction
# For each chamber, compute the image line and check transitions

# First, let's get the affine formulas for each chamber by fitting
from collections import defaultdict

# Group states by chamber and fit affine formulas
chamber_formulas = {}
for perm, states in chamber_groups.items():
    # Collect (g1, g2, g3) -> (g1', g2', g3') mappings
    X = []
    Y = []
    for g, raw in states:
        if g in transitions:
            img = transitions[g]
            X.append(list(g))
            Y.append(list(img))
    
    X = np.array(X)
    Y = np.array(Y)
    
    # Fit affine: Y = A*X + b (least squares, but should be exact)
    # Add column of ones for bias
    X_aug = np.hstack([X, np.ones((X.shape[0], 1))])
    
    # Solve for each output dimension
    coeffs = []
    for i in range(3):
        sol = np.linalg.lstsq(X_aug, Y[:, i], rcond=None)[0]
        coeffs.append(sol)
    
    A = np.array([c[:3] for c in coeffs])
    b = np.array([c[3] for c in coeffs])
    
    # Verify exactness
    max_error = 0
    for j, g in enumerate(X):
        pred = A @ g + b
        actual = Y[j]
        err = np.max(np.abs(pred - actual))
        if err > max_error:
            max_error = err
    
    chamber_formulas[perm] = (A, b, max_error)
    print(f"\nChamber {perm}:")
    print(f"  A =\n{A}")
    print(f"  b = {b}")
    print(f"  max_error = {max_error}")
    print(f"  rank = {np.linalg.matrix_rank(A)}")
    
    # Identify the line: image of A is span of some vector + b
    if np.linalg.matrix_rank(A) == 1:
        # Find the direction vector
        u, s, vh = np.linalg.svd(A)
        direction = u[:, 0]  # dominant left singular vector
        print(f"  line direction: {direction}")

# Now let's build the line transition graph
print("\n" + "="*60)
print("LINE TRANSITION GRAPH")
print("="*60)

line_transitions = defaultdict(set)
for perm, states in chamber_groups.items():
    A, b, _ = chamber_formulas[perm]
    # Identify the line for this chamber
    if np.linalg.matrix_rank(A) == 0:
        line_id = f"point_{perm}"
    else:
        u, s, vh = np.linalg.svd(A)
        direction = tuple(np.round(u[:, 0], 3))
        line_id = f"line_{direction}"
    
    for g, raw in states:
        if g in transitions:
            img = transitions[g]
            # Find which chamber the image belongs to
            img_chamber = classify_chamber(img)
            if img_chamber:
                img_perm = img_chamber[0]
                line_transitions[perm].add(img_perm)

for perm, targets in sorted(line_transitions.items()):
    print(f"Chamber {perm} -> {sorted(targets)}")Chamber (1, 2, 3, 0):

# Let's verify the 2D projection for boundary states (g1+g2+g3 = 9)
# and check if the 2D matrices have rank <= 1

boundary_states = []
for g1 in range(10):
    for g2 in range(10):
        g3 = 9 - g1 - g2
        if g3 >= 0 and g3 <= 9:
            boundary_states.append((g1, g2, g3))

print(f"Boundary states (sum=9): {len(boundary_states)}")

# Get transitions for boundary states
boundary_transitions = {}
for g in boundary_states:
    if g in transitions:
        boundary_transitions[g] = transitions[g]

print(f"Boundary transitions: {len(boundary_transitions)}")

# Group boundary states by chamber and fit 2D formulas (using g3 = 9 - g1 - g2)
boundary_chamber_groups = defaultdict(list)
for g in boundary_states:
    chamber_info = classify_chamber(g)
    if chamber_info:
        perm, raw = chamber_info
        if g in boundary_transitions:
            boundary_chamber_groups[perm].append((g, raw, boundary_transitions[g]))

print(f"\nBoundary chambers: {len(boundary_chamber_groups)}")
for perm, states in sorted(boundary_chamber_groups.items(), key=lambda x: len(x[1]), reverse=True):
    print(f"  Chamber {perm}: {len(states)} states")

# Fit 2D affine formulas: T(g1, g2) = A*(g1, g2) + b
print("\n" + "="*60)
print("2D AFFINE FORMULAS (Boundary States)")
print("="*60)

chamber_2d_formulas = {}
for perm, states in boundary_chamber_groups.items():
    X = []
    Y = []
    for g, raw, img in states:
        g1, g2, g3 = g
        X.append([g1, g2])
        Y.append([img[0], img[1]])  # g1', g2'
    
    X = np.array(X, dtype=float)
    Y = np.array(Y, dtype=float)
    
    # Fit: Y = A*X + b
    X_aug = np.hstack([X, np.ones((X.shape[0], 1))])
    coeffs = []
    for i in range(2):
        sol = np.linalg.lstsq(X_aug, Y[:, i], rcond=None)[0]
        coeffs.append(sol)
    
    A = np.array([c[:2] for c in coeffs])
    b = np.array([c[2] for c in coeffs])
    
    # Verify exactness
    max_error = 0
    for j, (g1, g2) in enumerate(X):
        pred = A @ [g1, g2] + b
        actual = Y[j]
        err = np.max(np.abs(pred - actual))
        if err > max_error:
            max_error = err
    
    chamber_2d_formulas[perm] = (A, b, max_error)
    
    # Round to integers for clean display
    A_rounded = np.round(A).astype(int)
    b_rounded = np.round(b).astype(int)
    
    print(f"\nChamber {perm}:")
    print(f"  A = {A_rounded.tolist()}")
    print(f"  b = {b_rounded.tolist()}")
    print(f"  max_error = {max_error:.2e}")
    print(f"  rank = {np.linalg.matrix_rank(A)}")
    print(f"  det = {np.linalg.det(A):.6f}")
    
    # Check if rank <= 1
    if np.linalg.matrix_rank(A) <= 1:
        u, s, vh = np.linalg.svd(A)
        direction = np.round(u[:, 0], 3)
        print(f"  ✓ Rank-1, direction: {direction}")
    else:
        print(f"  ✗ Rank 2")Boundary states (sum=9): 55

# Let's compute the depth classes
depth_classes = defaultdict(list)
for g in all_gaps:
    if g in transitions:
        orbit, status = trace_fixed(g)
        if "converged" in status:
            d = int(status.split("step ")[1])
            depth_classes[d].append(g)

print("Depth classes:")
for d in sorted(depth_classes.keys()):
    print(f"  depth {d}: {len(depth_classes[d])} states")

# Kernel classes: states that map to the same image
kernel_classes = defaultdict(list)
for g in all_gaps:
    if g in transitions:
        img = transitions[g]
        kernel_classes[img].append(g)

print(f"\nKernel classes: {len(kernel_classes)}")
